| TOUCH-1 | none | not applicable | netlist | No filter or supply capacitor connects a mains conductor to protective earth or to an accessible conductive part: the only parts on mains nets are the opto-triac output, triac, varistor and gate resistors, all between mains conductors. |
| INDEP-1 | none | not applicable | netlist (16 nets, 14 parts: no oscillator, timer, latch, reset net or reset output); circuit-proof.md 'Independence of the heater chain from the controller'; main-board circuit-verdicts.md (Whole board and Interlock rows, INDEP-1 PASS) and main-board schematic-review.md round 7 INDEP-1 rows for the K201 element; onsemi MOC3083M/D Rev. 2 (Sept 2022) p.1 Figure 1, p.4 (IFT 5 mA, IDRM1 500 nA) | Applies when the schematic carries an element that removes power from a hazardous load when the controller stops showing it is running. This board holds no watchdog-held switch, timer or latch; that element is the main board's K201 chain, judged on that board. Traced anyway, for the board's own chain (U911/U921, R911/R912, R921/R922, T911/T921), the three things the item names, separately. CLOCK: no net of the board reaches a clock, oscillator or timer output; the only controller signal is the command on /HEAT_C_LED_K and /HEAT_S_LED_K; with the controller's clock stopped, nothing on this board changes the command and no record shows the main board's sink gate returning to off, so a heater commanded on is not shown to be removed: the chain's default is not shown to be 'removes power' for a stopped clock, which is why it is not the element and why K201 is. RESET: no reset net or output; with the controller in reset the LED current is at most 5 uA (main board row, cited in circuit-proof.md row (c)) against IFT 5 mA max, so both triacs are held off, the direction that removes power. SUPPLY: the only supply is /+5V_WB, the main board's +5V through JP761, the rail the main board's records call the logic supply; below the LED's threshold the LED is unlit and the triac holds off (row (c), x4 to x13 on VGD, an estimate from typical curves); no logic runs on this board. A stuck-on cause begins on this board (FAULT-1), not in the clock, reset or supply. |
| EMSRC-2 | T911 | pass | ST DS2114 Rev 11 (May 2018) Table 4 p.3 (BW column: (dV/dt) 1000 V/us min, (dI/dt)c 14 A/ms min without snubber at 125 degC); circuit-proof.md row (c) closing arithmetic, W_margins.log (c) | Resistive element, zero-cross firing: re-applied slope 2 pi f x 358 V = 0.135 V/us at 60 Hz and 2.87 A/ms commutating at 5.38 A rms, against 1000 V/us and 14 A/ms (x4.9 on dI/dt); the sheet itself says no snubber is needed for the BW grade on inductive loads, and none is fitted. |

### Earlier findings, closure

Each earlier finding, read against the records, the graph and the documents at d9c2c49a. The designer writes Closed by; this table says whether each is valid under step 5 as the amended criteria SOL-HW-HEATER-WALL-BOARD-PROVEN-AND-LAID-OUT.C1 and DEL-HW-HEATER-WALL-BOARD-DESIGNED.C1 now read (a change naming its commit; `met:`, `does not apply:` or `overridden by:` naming a requirement or catalogue item; or the owner's recorded decision to carry the finding to a named later step, each carried finding naming a criterion that asks for it; for a safety finding a reason or carry closes only with a named person's `accepted_by:` and `accepted_on:` in the cell, never by the agent that drew or reviewed the board). Each named criterion was read with `show` this round.

| Finding | Closure verdict at d9c2c49a | Change needed |
|---|---|---|

C8 (read) admits an outcome that leaves the hazard bounded by a named element independent of the failed part and of the controller; the element's own series thermostats are that element, and W-6 names what they do not cover (a thermostat that resets; K201 only for a stopped controller or a collapsed rail; F101 not at all). ### Round 7 counts

93 verdict rows: 35 pass, 47 fail, 11 not applicable, 0 cannot judge (including one row for the free pass). 3 new findings, all raised by the free pass and all of quality severity: 7.1 (sheet source files still carry the maker-maximum Rating of the gate resistors), 7.2 (records still cite DEL-HW-BRING-UP.C13 for what C11 and C6 ask), 7.3 (records contradict W-3 and W-5c). Every other fail row cites an earlier finding. **Round 7 is not clean.** Reasons: (1) 7.1, 7.2 and 7.3 are open, and 6.1's closure is incomplete for 7.1; (2) of the earlier findings, valid are 5.1, 6.2, 1.1, 1.13, 2.10, 2.11, 3.2, 3.3, 1.11, 1.12, 1.2, 2.1, 3.1, 4.1, 1.8, 2.7 and, under the standing instruction for non-safety findings signed on the owner's behalf, 1.6, 2.5, 1.7, 2.6, 1.10, 2.9, 1.9, 2.8; not valid are 6.1 (sweep), 4.2 (Closed by form), 1.5 and 2.4 (the named criteria do not ask for the surge inputs or the series device) and, until David Baker's own acceptance of the carry is recorded, 1.3, 2.2, 1.4 and 2.3. The schematic itself is unchanged in net, part, value and footprint since round 4 and no circuit defect was found this round: every net and pin agrees with schematic-spec.yaml and the onsemi pinout, ERC is clean, the render is legible, and the key arithmetic and the new thermal arithmetic re-derive. What would make a next round clean: the four yaml strings and the three record corrections (all edits to records, not to the schematic), the 4.2 and 6.1 wording, a recorded acceptance by David Baker of the carry of the barrier and surge findings with the criterion for the surge inputs named, and the owner's confirmation of W-5a, W-5b and W-5d if the standing instruction is not taken as his decision. Not sourced by this round and recorded as unsourced in the records, none filled in here: the surge generator's source impedance and the household-appliance surge level, the entry varistor's clamp above 50 A, the 07D471K's clamp away from 10 A, the microsecond withstand of the triac, the opto-triac and the 1206 resistors, the 1206 resistor's pulse rating, the opto-triac's thermal resistance, whether the elements' thermostats reset, any rating of the push-on terminals and joiners and of the TE 63849-1 blade, a discharge level for the connectors, the cable resistance in finding 4.1, what an over-current LED does, the bolted joint's contact resistance Rc, the steel's grade and thickness, the idle-hold power of the heaters, and the temperature of the wall's outer face at the idle-hold power (the model gives the junction, not the touchable face; no record states it).

- **Round kind**: full (every item of the circuit practice catalogue against every net, part and sheet). A full round by a reviewer who did not draw the schematic and did not judge rounds 1 to 7; the one-reviewer form was used.
- **Schematic judged**: git commit 08d3fd23 (hardware/sunbeam-em7000/heater-wall-board/heater-wall-board.kicad_sch and its four sheets 100, 200, 300, 400). Changed since round 7 and judged here: the sheet yaml rating strings, the citation and wording corrections of commit 25dfba09, the Closed by cells (commit 08d3fd23) and owner-sign-off.md W-8.
- **Netlist**: `/Applications/KiCad/KiCad.app/Contents/MacOS/kicad-cli sch export netlist --output review.net hardware/sunbeam-em7000/heater-wall-board/heater-wall-board.kicad_sch` (KiCad 10.0.7), and the same command with `--format kicadxml` for a script. No line is a schematic finding.
- **Arithmetic re-derived in round 8** (each agrees with the records): 357.8 V / 396 ohm = 0.904 A against ITSM 1 A (x1.11); RCWV = sqrt(0.25 W x 200 ohm) = 7.07 V and overload voltage 2.5 x 7.07 = 17.7 V; 178.9 V per resistor is 25 times the RCWV and 10 times the overload voltage; firing at 0.05 A x 404 ohm + 3.0 V + 1.3 V = 24.5 V of line voltage, 24.5 V / 52.7 ohm = 0.465 A against IL 70 mA (x6.6), 4.6 degrees after the crossing at 216 V rms; the LED limit (4.75 - 1.5) / 0.060 = 54.2 ohm and (5.25 - 1.5) / 0.060 = 62.5 ohm; 253^2 / (4 x 47) = 340.5 W in the varistor alone and as much in the element (2.7 A rms); 253^2 / 47 ohm x 10 ms = 13.6 J; the triac thermal cells from the division record's own equation T_j = T_a + P x (R_path + 0.5325 x 2.6) with R_path = 6.30 + 0.166 K/W (h = 10) or 9.32 + 0.166 K/W (h = 5): both triacs 9.09 W, 96.4, 111.4, 123.8 and 138.8 degC; steam alone 4.84 W, 68.9, 83.9, 83.5 and 98.5 degC; the Rc rows (105.5, 123.6, 120.5, 138.6, 132.9, 151.1 degC) and the cases 86.2 and 101.2 degC; W_margins.py re-run identical to its log.
- **Drawings**: `kicad-cli sch export pdf` of the clone, `pdftoppm -r 80`, all five sheets read as images (the root sheet with its four sheet symbols and pins, the coffee and steam sheets left to right from the LED return and +5V_WB to the triac and the blade, the header sheet and the return sheet): every net named, no overlapping text, pins numbered, the opto-triac pins 4 and 6 and the triac pins 1, 2 and 3 as the netlist holds them.

| TOUCH-1 | none | not applicable | netlist | No filter or supply capacitor connects a mains conductor to protective earth or to an accessible conductive part: the only parts on mains nets are the opto-triac output, triac, varistor and gate resistors, all between mains conductors. |
| INDEP-1 | none | not applicable | netlist (16 nets, 14 parts: no oscillator, timer, latch, reset net or reset output); circuit-proof.md 'Independence of the heater chain from the controller'; main-board circuit-verdicts.md (Whole board and Interlock rows, INDEP-1 PASS) and main-board schematic-review.md round 7 INDEP-1 rows for the K201 element; onsemi MOC3083M/D Rev. 2 (Sept 2022) p.1 Figure 1, p.4 (IFT 5 mA, IDRM1 500 nA) | Applies when the schematic carries an element that removes power from a hazardous load when the controller stops showing it is running. This board holds no watchdog-held switch, timer or latch; that element is the main board's K201 chain, judged on that board. Traced anyway, for the board's own chain (U911/U921, R911/R912, R921/R922, T911/T921), the three things the item names, separately. CLOCK: no net of the board reaches a clock, oscillator or timer output; the only controller signal is the command on /HEAT_C_LED_K and /HEAT_S_LED_K; with the controller's clock stopped, nothing on this board changes the command and no record shows the main board's sink gate returning to off, so a heater commanded on is not shown to be removed: the chain's default is not shown to be 'removes power' for a stopped clock, which is why it is not the element and why K201 is. RESET: no reset net or output; with the controller in reset the LED current is at most 5 uA (main board row, cited in circuit-proof.md row (c)) against IFT 5 mA max, so both triacs are held off, the direction that removes power. SUPPLY: the only supply is /+5V_WB, the main board's +5V through JP761, the rail the main board's records call the logic supply; below the LED's threshold the LED is unlit and the triac holds off (row (c), x4 to x13 on VGD, an estimate from typical curves); no logic runs on this board. A stuck-on cause begins on this board (FAULT-1), not in the clock, reset or supply. |
| EMSRC-2 | T911 | pass | ST DS2114 Rev 11 (May 2018) Table 4 p.3 (BW column: (dV/dt) 1000 V/us min, (dI/dt)c 14 A/ms min without snubber at 125 degC); circuit-proof.md row (c) closing arithmetic, W_margins.log (c) | Resistive element, zero-cross firing: re-applied slope 2 pi f x 358 V = 0.135 V/us at 60 Hz and 2.87 A/ms commutating at 5.38 A rms, against 1000 V/us and 14 A/ms (x4.9 on dI/dt); the sheet itself says no snubber is needed for the BW grade on inductive loads, and none is fitted. |
| EMSRC-2 | T921 | pass | ST DS2114 Rev 11 (May 2018) Table 4 p.3 (BW column: (dV/dt) 1000 V/us min, (dI/dt)c 14 A/ms min without snubber at 125 degC); circuit-proof.md row (c) closing arithmetic, W_margins.log (c) | Resistive element, zero-cross firing: re-applied slope 2 pi f x 358 V = 0.135 V/us at 60 Hz and 2.87 A/ms commutating at 5.38 A rms, against 1000 V/us and 14 A/ms (x4.9 on dI/dt); the sheet itself says no snubber is needed for the BW grade on inductive loads, and none is fitted. |

| DERATE-2 | T911 | fail | ST DS2114 Rev 11 (May 2018) Table 1 p.2 (IT(RMS) 16 A at Tc 86 degC, Tj 125 degC), Table 5 p.3 (Rth(j-c) 2.1 K/W), Figure 3 p.4; circuit-proof.md row (e) and grading table, W_margins.log (e); derating-policy.yaml triac; docs/sunbeam-em7000/controller-board-division.md 'The heater wall assembly, judged', 2026-11-19 amendment (equation, R_wall, the table); owner-sign-off.md W-5c | 2.7 (earlier finding, carried by David Baker's own words W-5c, 2026-11-19, to DEL-HW-BRING-UP.C11 and DEL-HW-HEATER-WALL-BOARD-DESIGNED.C22). Re-derived on the 2026-11-19 re-basing from the record's own equation Tj = Ta + P x (Rwall + Rbracket + Rfilm + Rc) + Psteam x (0.50 + 2.1), Psteam = 0.5325 P, Rwall 6.30 (h 10) or 9.32 (h 5) K/W, Rbracket + Rfilm 0.166 K/W: both triacs 9.09 W at 25 degC, h 10, Rc 0: 25 + 9.09 x 6.466 + 4.84 x 2.6 = 96.4 degC; 40 degC 111.4; h 5 123.8 and 138.8; steam alone 25 + 4.84 x 9.066 + 12.6 = 68.9 (40 degC 83.9). Case = Tj - 4.84 x 2.1 = 86.2 degC (25 degC) and 101.2 degC (40 degC); current share 7.95 A at 25 degC and 4.88 to 5.46 A at 40 degC (Rc 0) against the steam element's 5.38 A, 3.0 to 3.7 A at 40 degC with Rc 1 K/W; the 100 degC share holds for both triacs only at 25 degC, h 10, Rc 0 (96.4 degC). Every cell of W_margins.log (e) amendment agrees with this arithmetic. Rc is an unsourced assumption and the steel's grade and thickness are unmeasured, so the finding stands as a carried one. |

*Suggestion, not a finding.* The touchable outer face of the machine's steel wall: at the contact patch the wall stands about P x R_wall above the external air, 9.09 W x 6.30 K/W = 57 K at continuous full power on both triacs at h = 10 (82 degC at 25 degC air), which is the design bound and not the duty (full power under a minute per the owner; idle-hold power unsourced). No record states the outer-face temperature at any duty. The owner's cut and bring-up thermocouples (DEL-HW-BRING-UP.C11) are where it would be read; if it is to be a requirement it needs a criterion, which round 7 also noted.

### Round 8 counts

93 verdict rows: 35 pass, 47 fail, 11 not applicable, 0 cannot judge (including one row for the free pass). 3 new findings, all raised by the free pass and all of quality severity: 8.1 (records still say the owner is to sign the W-3 residual; 7.3(a)'s sweep incomplete), 8.2 (criteria cited for what they do not ask, and records saying other records cite what they do not), 8.3 (W-5c contradicts itself on what the owner accepted against). Every other fail row cites an earlier finding. **Round 8 is not clean.** Reasons: (1) 8.1, 8.2 and 8.3 are open; (2) of the earlier findings, 7.2 and 7.3 are not validly closed (no commit named, sweeps incomplete), 7.1 and 6.1 lack only the commit name for the 25dfba09 corrections; (3) the surge and barrier carries (1.3, 2.2, 1.4, 2.3, 1.5, 2.4) are now valid, closed by David Baker's recorded 'Yes' (W-8) to criteria that ask for them, and 4.2 is valid. The schematic itself is unchanged in net, part, value, footprint and field since round 4 and no circuit defect was found: netlist equals spec, ERC 0, all 14 drawn field sets equal parts-specification.yaml, the five sheets render clean, the key arithmetic and the thermal cells re-derive, and the pre-order check's lines for the wall are all layout-time except four ACCEPTED derating exceptions. What would make a next round clean: the wording and record corrections above (no change to the schematic) and, for 8.3, the owner's word on what he was shown. Findings that remain carried to later steps once closed: 1.3, 2.2, 1.4, 2.3 (basis re-declaration before ordering, SOL-HW-HEATER-WALL-BOARD-PROVEN-AND-LAID-OUT.C5 and SOL-HW-SEPARATION-BASIS-SIGNED.C2; earth bond, DEL-HW-BRING-UP.C6); 1.5, 2.4 (REQ-ELECTRICAL-INSTALL-001.C10 and REQ-ACTUATION-CONFIRMATION-001.C10); 1.6, 2.5 (C24 and REQ-ELECTRICAL-INSTALL-001.C11, on his behalf); 1.7, 2.6, 1.10, 2.9 (C20, C15, on his behalf); 1.8, 2.7 (C11, C22); 1.9, 2.8 (C11, C13, on his behalf). Not sourced, recorded as unsourced and none filled in here: the surge generator's source impedance and the household-appliance surge level, the entry varistor's clamp above 50 A, the 07D471K's clamp away from 10 A, the microsecond withstand of the triac, the opto-triac and the 1206 resistors, the 1206 resistor's pulse rating, the opto-triac's thermal resistance, whether the elements' thermostats reset, any rating of the push-on terminals, joiners and of the TE 63849-1 blade, a discharge level for the connectors, the cable resistance in finding 4.1, what an over-current LED does, the bolted joint's contact resistance, the steel's grade and thickness, the idle-hold power and warm-up time of the heaters, and the temperature of the wall's outer face.

Changed since round 8 and judged here: commits 87b592cd (round 8 record, the corrections for findings 8.1 to 8.3 and 7.2 and 7.3, sweep-log.md, the outer-face statement) and 94f0534b (the commit named in the Closed by cells of 7.2 and 7.3).

- **Arithmetic re-derived in round 9** (each agrees with the records): 357.8 V / 396 ohm = 0.904 A against ITSM 1 A (x1.11); RCWV = sqrt(0.25 W x 200 ohm) = 7.07 V, overload voltage 2.5 x 7.07 = 17.7 V, 178.9 V per resistor is 25 times the RCWV and 10 times the overload voltage, 10.1 V at a firing is x1.43 and 0.505 W is twice the 250 mW; firing at 0.05 A x 404 ohm + 3.0 V + 1.3 V = 24.5 V of line voltage, 4.6 degrees after the crossing at 216 V rms, 24.5 V / 52.7 ohm = 0.465 A against IL 70 mA (x6.6; the record writes 0.46 A and x6.6, the same figure); the LED limit (4.75 - 1.5) / 0.060 = 54.2 ohm and (5.25 - 1.5) / 0.060 = 62.5 ohm, and every row of the LED-return table; the emitter power 120 - 1.41 x 45 = 56.6 mW at 70 degC, 37.7 mA at VF 1.5 V; 253^2 / (4 x 47) = 340.5 W in the varistor alone and as much in the element (2.69 A rms, 681 W together); 253^2 / 47 ohm x 10 ms = 13.6 J; 775 V / 396 ohm = 1.96 A and 387.5 V, 758 W per resistor; the clamp 25 V (3.1 %) under 800 V; the triac thermal cells from the division record's own equation T_j = T_a + P x (R_wall + 0.166 + R_c) + 0.5325 x P x 2.6 with R_wall 6.30 K/W (h = 10) or 9.32 K/W (h = 5): both triacs 9.09 W, 96.4, 111.4, 123.8 and 138.8 degC; steam alone 4.84 W, 68.9, 83.9, 83.5 and 98.5 degC and, with R_c, 73.7, 88.7, 88.4, 103.4, 83.4, 98.4, 98.0 and 113.0 degC as the division record's table gives them; the Rc rows of W_margins.log (105.5, 123.6, 120.5, 138.6, 132.9, 151.1 degC), the cases 86.2 and 101.2 degC (junction less 4.84 x 2.1 K/W) and the current shares 7.95 A at 25 degC and 4.88 to 5.46 A at 40 degC; the opto-triac cells (340 K/W implied, 10.7 K self-rise, 15 K, 7.3 K, 32.3 K, 27.3 K); the outer face: 9.09 W x 6.30 K/W = 57.3 K, 82 degC over 25 degC air, 97 degC over 40 degC air, and 9.09 x 9.32 = 84.7 K at h = 5 (the equation as the record states it, and not an independent measure of the wall's outer face).
- **Drawings**: `kicad-cli sch export pdf` of the clone, `pdftoppm -r 90`, all five sheets read as images (the root sheet with its four sheet symbols and pins, the coffee and steam sheets left to right from the LED return and +5V_WB to the triac and the blade, the header sheet and the return sheet): every net named, no overlapping text, pins numbered, the opto-triac pins 4 and 6 and the triac pins 1, 2 and 3 as the netlist holds them.

| TOUCH-1 | none | not applicable | netlist | No filter or supply capacitor connects a mains conductor to protective earth or to an accessible conductive part: the only parts on mains nets are the opto-triac output, triac, varistor and gate resistors, all between mains conductors. |
| INDEP-1 | none | not applicable | netlist (16 nets, 14 parts: no oscillator, timer, latch, reset net or reset output); circuit-proof.md 'Independence of the heater chain from the controller'; main-board circuit-verdicts.md (Whole board and Interlock rows, INDEP-1 PASS) and main-board schematic-review.md round 7 INDEP-1 rows for the K201 element; onsemi MOC3083M/D Rev. 2 (Sept 2022) p.1 Figure 1, p.4 (IFT 5 mA, IDRM1 500 nA) | Applies when the schematic carries an element that removes power from a hazardous load when the controller stops showing it is running. This board holds no watchdog-held switch, timer or latch; that element is the main board's K201 chain, judged on that board. Traced anyway, for the board's own chain (U911/U921, R911/R912, R921/R922, T911/T921), the three things the item names, separately. CLOCK: no net of the board reaches a clock, oscillator or timer output; the only controller signal is the command on /HEAT_C_LED_K and /HEAT_S_LED_K; with the controller's clock stopped, nothing on this board changes the command and no record shows the main board's sink gate returning to off, so a heater commanded on is not shown to be removed: the chain's default is not shown to be 'removes power' for a stopped clock, which is why it is not the element and why K201 is. RESET: no reset net or output; with the controller in reset the LED current is at most 5 uA (main board row, cited in circuit-proof.md row (c)) against IFT 5 mA max, so both triacs are held off, the direction that removes power. SUPPLY: the only supply is /+5V_WB, the main board's +5V through JP761, the rail the main board's records call the logic supply; below the LED's threshold the LED is unlit and the triac holds off (row (c), x4 to x13 on VGD, an estimate from typical curves); no logic runs on this board. A stuck-on cause begins on this board (FAULT-1), not in the clock, reset or supply. |
| EMSRC-2 | T911 | pass | ST DS2114 Rev 11 (May 2018) Table 4 p.3 (BW column: (dV/dt) 1000 V/us min, (dI/dt)c 14 A/ms min without snubber at 125 degC); circuit-proof.md row (c) closing arithmetic, W_margins.log (c) | Resistive element, zero-cross firing: re-applied slope 2 pi f x 358 V = 0.135 V/us at 60 Hz and 2.87 A/ms commutating at 5.38 A rms, against 1000 V/us and 14 A/ms (x4.9 on dI/dt); the sheet itself says no snubber is needed for the BW grade on inductive loads, and none is fitted. |

| DERATE-2 | T911 | fail | ST DS2114 Rev 11 (May 2018) Table 1 p.2 (IT(RMS) 16 A at Tc 86 degC, Tj 125 degC), Table 5 p.3 (Rth(j-c) 2.1 K/W), Figure 3 p.4; circuit-proof.md row (e) and grading table, W_margins.log (e); derating-policy.yaml triac; docs/sunbeam-em7000/controller-board-division.md 'The heater wall assembly, judged', 2026-11-19 amendment (equation, R_wall, the table); owner-sign-off.md W-5c | 2.7 (earlier finding, carried by David Baker's own words W-5c, 2026-11-19, to DEL-HW-BRING-UP.C11 and DEL-HW-HEATER-WALL-BOARD-DESIGNED.C22). Re-derived on the 2026-11-19 re-basing from the record's own equation Tj = Ta + P x (Rwall + Rbracket + Rfilm + Rc) + Psteam x (0.50 + 2.1), Psteam = 0.5325 P, Rwall 6.30 (h 10) or 9.32 (h 5) K/W, Rbracket + Rfilm 0.166 K/W: both triacs 9.09 W at 25 degC, h 10, Rc 0: 25 + 9.09 x 6.466 + 4.84 x 2.6 = 96.4 degC; 40 degC 111.4; h 5 123.8 and 138.8; steam alone 25 + 4.84 x 9.066 + 12.6 = 68.9 (40 degC 83.9). Case = Tj - 4.84 x 2.1 = 86.2 degC (25 degC) and 101.2 degC (40 degC); current share 7.95 A at 25 degC and 4.88 to 5.46 A at 40 degC (Rc 0) against the steam element's 5.38 A, 3.0 to 3.7 A at 40 degC with Rc 1 K/W; the 100 degC share holds for both triacs only at 25 degC, h 10, Rc 0 (96.4 degC). Every cell of W_margins.log (e) amendment agrees with this arithmetic. Rc is an unsourced assumption and the steel's grade and thickness are unmeasured, so the finding stands as a carried one. |

### Round 9 counts

93 verdict rows: 35 pass, 47 fail, 11 not applicable, 0 cannot judge (including one row for the free pass). 4 new findings, all raised by the free pass and all of quality severity: 9.1 (owner-sign-off.md's sections and headings misstate who accepted what and when, and 'the only words of his' is false), 9.2 (open-items.md line 3 and the generated W_margins record misstate the acceptances), 9.3 (340 W stated ambiguously in circuit-proof.md after 7.3(b) corrected it elsewhere), 9.4 (the outer-face temperature said to be on an end-of-work list that does not carry it). Every other fail row cites an earlier finding. **Round 9 is not clean.** Reasons: (1) 9.1 to 9.4 are open; (2) 8.3 is not validly closed (what David Baker was shown is asserted with no source and the commit history shows both lists were in the record before he replied); (3) 7.3's (b) class sweep is incomplete (9.3). The schematic itself is unchanged in net, part, value, footprint and field since round 4 and no circuit defect was found: netlist equals spec, ERC 0, all 14 drawn field sets equal parts-specification.yaml, the five sheets render clean, the key arithmetic and the thermal cells re-derive, and the pre-order check's wall lines are layout-time except four ACCEPTED derating exceptions. What would make round 10 clean: record corrections only, no change to the schematic (the four findings, and 8.3 by the owner's own line or by reducing the statement to what the record evidences), each with its class sweep recorded in sweep-log.md. Findings that remain carried to later steps once closed: 1.3, 2.2, 1.4, 2.3 (basis re-declaration before ordering, SOL-HW-HEATER-WALL-BOARD-PROVEN-AND-LAID-OUT.C5 and SOL-HW-SEPARATION-BASIS-SIGNED.C2; earth bond, DEL-HW-BRING-UP.C6; his words); 1.5, 2.4 (REQ-ELECTRICAL-INSTALL-001.C10 and REQ-ACTUATION-CONFIRMATION-001.C10; his words); 1.6, 2.5 (C24 and REQ-ELECTRICAL-INSTALL-001.C11, on his behalf); 1.7, 2.6, 1.10, 2.9 (C20, C15, on his behalf); 1.8, 2.7 (C11, C22; his words); 1.9, 2.8 (C11, C13, on his behalf). Not sourced, recorded as unsourced and none filled in here: the surge generator's source impedance and the household-appliance surge level, the entry varistor's clamp above 50 A, the 07D471K's clamp away from 10 A, the microsecond withstand of the triac, the opto-triac and the 1206 resistors, the 1206 resistor's pulse rating, the opto-triac's thermal resistance, whether the elements' thermostats reset, any rating of the push-on terminals, joiners and of the TE 63849-1 blade, a discharge level for the connectors, the cable resistance in finding 4.1, what an over-current LED does, the bolted joint's contact resistance, the steel's grade and thickness, the idle-hold power and warm-up time of the heaters, the temperature of the wall's outer face and any limit for it, and the triac package's creepage, clearance and impulse figures.
